- **study-sources**: Reads the real source of a library, framework or SDK at the version the project resolved, so code is written against the actual API instead of a remembered one. Use this skill before writing code that calls a dependency API you have not read in this repo; whenever a compile or type error names a library symbol (Unresolved reference, has no exported member, cannot find symbol, has no attribute, is not a function); when a stack trace leads into node_modules, site-packages or a cached JAR; when a version may be newer than you know; or when the user asks how a library works or to pull down a repo… ([jayteealao/agent-skills](https://github.com/jayteealao/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-workflow/skills/study-sources))
